# H3500 programs

Every program of the H3500 ROM set this project builds (OS 2.17 with all
five program ROMs: 780 programs), with the soft key parameters of each
parameter page, its Soft Functions and its modulation patches, which include
its MIDI controller assignments.

This was generated by loading each program in the emulator
(`emu/src/bin/h3000-host.rs`, command `report`; formatted by
`tools/programs_report.py`) and reading the front panel, so it shows what
the original firmware shows, abbreviations included. The factory state
applies: every program as it comes out of the ROMs, MIDI on all channels.

## Calling programs up over MIDI

With the factory MIDI setup (SETUP, "MIDI Program Change Functions":
Pgm=ID, Pgm=On, Bank=0) a program change selects a program by its number:

- Program change 100-127 loads program 100-127, whatever the bank.
- Program change 0-99 loads program *bank* x 100 + *n*: with Bank=2,
  program change 22 loads 222. The bank is set on that SETUP page only;
  MIDI bank select (CC 0 / CC 32) is ignored.
- Pgm=Map instead uses the MIDI map table (the Map soft key on the same
  page), which is empty in the factory state.

Each program below lists the program change that calls it up. Changing
programs from the front panel is described in README.md.

## Reading the entries

- **Soft keys**: the four soft key labels of each parameter page, in the
  order PAGE> steps through them. Labels such as `quan=off` show a
  setting's state; `levels` and `expert` lead to further pages (input /
  output levels, expert parameters), which are not listed.
- **Soft Functions**: the H3500's four per-program control knobs (name,
  sensitivity, polarity), shown when the program defines them. A patch
  routes a Soft Function to one or more parameters.
- **Patches**: each parameter with a modulation source assigned in the
  program. MIDI sources respond on the base MIDI channel (SETUP, "MIDI
  Functions", Base=1) plus the offset shown; *range* is the modulation
  depth in the parameter's units, *per note* the extra depth per MIDI
  note for note-dependent sources.

406 programs have patches; 90 of them respond to MIDI controllers, wheels,
pedals or notes. Sources used, with the number of patches:

| Source | Patches |
|---|---|
| Soft Function 1 | 451 |
| Soft Function 2 | 340 |
| Soft Function 4 | 305 |
| Soft Function 3 | 253 |
| Function Generator | 220 |
| Damper Pedal (base channel) | 55 |
| Modulation Wheel (base channel) | 24 |
| Pitch Wheel only (base channel) | 10 |
| MIDI Clock Period | 8 |
| Foot Controller (base channel) | 5 |
| (unreadable: the display shows ROM bytes) | 4 |
| Global Pressure (base channel) | 4 |
| Last Note &  PW=   7.0 (base channel) | 4 |
| Notes Down    Th=  1 (base channel) | 4 |
| Last Note &  PW=    .0 (base channel) | 3 |
| Last Note Velocity (base channel) | 3 |
| Pitch Wheel Positive (base channel) | 3 |
| Foot Controller (base channel + 1) | 2 |
| GP Controller # 2 (base channel) | 2 |
| GP Controller # 6 (base channel) | 2 |
| GP Controller # 7 (base channel) | 2 |
| Undefined Small=  83 (base channel) | 2 |
| Function Gen. Th= 80 Up | 1 |
| GP Controller # 1 (base channel) | 1 |
| GP Controller # 3 (base channel) | 1 |
| GP Controller # 4 (base channel) | 1 |
| GP Controller # 5 (base channel) | 1 |
| High Note &  PW=    .0 (base channel) | 1 |
| Last Note (base channel) | 1 |
| Note      G#1 above (base channel + 2) | 1 |
| Note Offset  PW=   5.8 (base channel) | 1 |
| Notes Down    Th=  4 (base channel) | 1 |
| Pitch Wheel only (base channel + 2) | 1 |
| unnamed source 0 (base channel) | 1 |

The H3500 names controllers by their standard MIDI meaning:

| H3500 source | MIDI message |
|---|---|
| Modulation Wheel | CC 1 |
| Foot Controller | CC 4 |
| Damper Pedal | CC 64 (sustain) |
| GP Controller # 1-4 / # 5-8 | CC 16-19 / CC 80-83 (general purpose) |
| Global Pressure | channel aftertouch |
| Pitch Wheel only / Pitch Wheel Positive | pitch bend (full / upward half) |
| Last Note, High Note, Note Offset, Notes Down, Last Note Velocity, Note *n* | note on / off |
| MIDI Clock Period | the tempo of incoming MIDI clock |

Other names (e.g. `Undefined Small= 83`) are as the H3500 displays them.
Function Generator sources are the H3500's internal LFO / envelope
(SETUP, "Modulation of Parameters", FuncGen), not MIDI.
